個股報告 
鮮活果汁-KY(1256) 
步入旺季，獲利上修 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $280 
前次投資建議：2025.12.31： 
「逢低買進」 ，目標價: $180

報告日期：2026.6.1 研 究 員：陳泂暉

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 公司果汁業務 26Q1 由 HPP直營通路帶動預包裝產品，營收占45.8%創首季新高；果粒業務
比重調至 26.2%，轉型創新協同角色，融合直飲研發以提升溢價空間。 
➢ 考量到擴充產能帶動營收成長，所以取區間中緣 16X，上調至「買進」投資評等，6 個月目
標價 280(16X2026(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 36.25%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/5/29 收盤價： 205.50 ➢ 公司 26Q1 營收 11.42億元，QoQ+30%、YoY+55.3%，
過往第一季以及第四季為傳統淡季，因為屬於冬季，但今
年受惠於楊枝甘露等預包裝產品帶動營收成長；毛利率
30.4%，QoQ+5.8ppts、YoY+6.4ppts，受惠於產品組合
改善，以及營收成長動規模效應；稅後淨利 1.16 億元，
QoQ+72.9%、YoY+371.8%；稅後 EPS 3.24 元。。 
➢ 公司引進 HPP技術打入直營通路，26Q1 營收占 45.8%，
憑預包裝產品帶動首季營收創歷史新高。 
➢ 果粒業務 26Q1 營收比重降至 26.2%，現轉型創新協同角
色，融合直飲成品研發以提升溢價空間。 
➢ 屏東新廠預計 2027 年 2月投產，定位為全球化擴張示範
點，服務本土超商零售並支援客戶布局美、東南亞市場。 
➢ 昆山新廠 2026 年 7 月運作，引進自動化線擴充 HPP 產
能，並透過智能系統建立彈性模組，兼顧客製與量產需
求，預估年產能 2萬噸。 
➢ 預估 2026 年營收 52.74 億元 ，YoY+28%； 毛利率30.8%，
YoY+3.1ppts；稅後淨利 6.18 億元，YoY+61.7%；稅後
EPS 17.28元。 
鮮活果汁目前股價位於 11.5X26(F)PER，位於公司過往
11-21X 區間下緣位置，考量到擴充產能帶動營收成長，所
以取區間中緣 16X，上調至「買進」投資評等，6個月目標
價 280(16X2026(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 36.25%。

營 運 分 析 
                   鮮活果汁為中國茶飲綜合解決方案原料供應商 
鮮活果汁-KY創立於 2001 年 1月，註冊地位於開曼群島，於2012 年 9月掛
牌上櫃並於 2016 年 3月轉上市，為大中華區指標性之「茶飲綜合解決方案」
原料供應商。公司專注於果汁、果粒及果粉類產品的研發、生產與銷售，憑
藉高度客製化研發能力與全流程服務體系，供應餐飲業及手搖飲門店調製飲
品之核心原料，或作為食品加工廠生產水果風味食品之關鍵添加原料。公司
2026年第一季營收比：果汁 71.9%、果粒 26.6%以及其他 1.5%。

圖一、鮮活果汁 26年第一季產品營收比重 
26年第一季產品營收比重

資料來源：公司法說會；兆豐國際彙整

公司 26Q1 營收 11.42 億元，QoQ+30%、YoY+55.3%，過往第一季以及第
四季為傳統淡季，因為屬於冬季，但今年受惠於楊枝甘露等預包裝產品帶動
營收成長；毛利率 30.4%，QoQ+5.8ppts、YoY+6.4ppts，受惠於產品組合
改善，以及營收成長動規模效應 ；稅後淨利 1.16 億元， QoQ+72.9%、
YoY+371.8%；稅後 EPS 3.24 元。

- 3 - 
個股報告 
HPP 技術賦能直飲業務，直攻高階零售戰場，帶動果汁營收成長 
果汁與預包裝直飲業務目前是鮮活果汁-KY 營收成長的最主要動能，策略核
心已從單純提供手搖飲原料，轉向預包裝直飲產品與零售新通路。主要受惠
於以下三點： 
1. 鮮活果汁積極引進 HPP 超高壓滅菌設備 。 此技術能完整保留果汁最真實
的風味與天然營養，在無  HPP 技術下鮮活已能將冷鏈保存期做到  21 
天（三個禮拜） ，導入 HPP 技術後更進一步將保存期延長至 4 到 5 個
禮拜。這使得產品得以成功進入盒馬鮮生、山姆會員店等高端冷鏈雙超通
路，解決了傳統手搖飲原料過去無法規模化零售的痛點。 
2. 受惠於預包裝冷鏈直飲產品成功打入山姆會員商店、盒馬鮮生與奧樂齊
（ALDI）等直營零售新通路，直營渠道的營收占比已從 2025 年全年的 
23.2%，於 26Q1 快速飆升至 45.8%，接近整體營收的半數。 
3. 公司透過廣西百色廠就近收購並初加工芒果等新鮮水果，配合全流程數位
化冷鏈運輸，打造出年營收突破人民幣 2 億元 （折合新台幣逾 10 億元）
的明星單品「楊枝甘露」。這類「即開即飲」的高階預包裝產品，其毛利
率顯著優於受手搖飲品牌自建工廠擠壓的傳統原料，並打破傳統冬季淡季
限制，帶動 26Q1 營收與毛利創下歷史首季新高。

圖二、中國餐飲業產業規模 (單位:人民幣億元) 
中國產業產業規模

資料來源：中國國家統計局、公司法說會；兆豐國際彙整

果粒業務之結構調整與協同角色轉變 
果粒業務（涵蓋果粒醬、晶球、魔芋顆粒等）曾為鮮活果汁的利潤重心。隨
產業環境變遷，目前該業務正經歷營收佔比的結構性調整，並邁入策略性的
功能轉型階段。

果粒類產品的營收在 2022 年之前曾大於果汁類產品。然而，隨著市場競爭
果粒業務之結構調整
與協同角色轉變 
HPP 技術 賦能直飲
業務，直攻高階零售
戰場，帶動果汁營收
成長

- 4 - 
個股報告 
內捲、精緻複合直飲產品（如：楊枝甘露、多肉葡萄）熱賣，以及公司資源
向新零售「直飲產品」傾斜，果粒類產品的營收佔比正在逐步下降，自25Q1
的 32%，到 26Q1 已調整至 26.2%，儘管佔比下滑，鮮活仍持續投入核心技
術研發，於 2023 年攻克「造球技術」 （晶球研發） ，2025 年引進「麥高球
技術」 ，並在近期創新推出具備趣味「跳的造型」口感晶球投入市場銷售，旨
在維持並提升在大型連鎖茶飲品牌（如：蜜雪冰城、古茗、CoCo 都可）中
的供應鏈技術黏著度。

目前果粒業務更多是扮演關鍵的「協同與創新角色」 。鮮活不再單純販售桶裝
顆粒原料，而是將獨家的口感顆粒技術與新鮮原汁、植物基底完美融合，直
接封裝進高階冷鏈直飲果汁成品中（如：明星單品楊枝甘露內含的口感顆粒
與穩定系統） ，透過豐富的口感層次與技術壁壘，全面提升產品的整體單價與
溢價空間。

圖三、公司近五年產品占比變化(%) 
公司近五年產品占比變化(%)

資料來源：公司法說會；兆豐國際彙整

深耕台灣、佈局全球，屏東新廠預計 2027年 2月正式投產 
台灣擴產計畫以屏東新廠為核心戰略基地，公司已於2025 年 10月完成廠房
收購 ， 目前正加速進行高階設備進場 、 環境優化及相關國際認證 ， 預計於2027
年 2月正式投產。該廠房定位為「全球化擴張的示範點」 ，除服務台灣本土連
鎖超商與大型零售通路外，更將發揮「台灣製造」的品牌優勢，支援客戶布
局東南亞及美國等海外市場，同時作為集團培養國際化管理人才的搖籃。

昆山新廠擴大 HPP產能，衝刺大陸直飲市場成長 
中國擴產計畫聚焦於智能化升級與產品線結構優化，公司計畫於昆山總部旁
昆山新廠擴大HPP產
能，衝刺大陸直飲市
場成長 
深耕台灣、佈局全
球，屏東新廠預計
2027年 2月正式投產

- 5 - 
個股報告 
增建新廠房，並引進 PET寶特瓶自動化生產線，預計於 2026 年 7月落成運
作，並預計貢獻年產能2萬噸。此計畫重點在於持續擴充HPP（超高壓殺菌）
產能以支撐直飲產品業務成長，並透過整合 MES 與 WMS 系統建立「彈性
生產模組」 ，使其具備隨意拼接產線的能力，藉此兼顧客製化小量訂單與大規
模量產需求，在優化人均產值的同時，進一步穩固在大陸新茶飲市場的技術
領先地位。

26Q2 喜迎傳統旺季、全年獲利看增，產能效益全面彰顯 
預估 26Q2 營收 14.81 億元，QoQ+29.7%、YoY+39.5%，受惠於傳統旺季
影響；預估毛利率 31.2%，QoQ+0.8ppts、YoY+0.6ppts，受惠產能利用率
提高；稅後淨利 1.81 億元，QoQ+56.6%、YoY+48.3%；稅後 EPS 5.07 元。

預估 2026 年營收 52.74 億元，YoY+28%；毛利率 30.8%，YoY+3.1ppts；
稅後淨利 6.18 億元，YoY+61.7%；稅後 EPS 17.28 元。

鮮活果汁目前股價位於 11.5X26(F)PER，位於公司過往 11-21X 區間下緣位
置，考量到擴充產能帶動營收成長，所以取區間中緣 16X，上調至「買進」
投資評等，6個月目標價 280(16X2026(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 36.25%。

公司簡介 
台聚成立於 1965 年 ， 為國內第二大的石化
集 團 ， 主 要 生 產 產 品 為 高 密 度 聚 乙 烯
（HDPE） ，線型低密度聚乙烯（LLDPE），
低密度聚乙烯樹脂 （LDPE Resin） 及乙烯醋
酸乙烯酯共聚合樹脂 （EVA Resin） ， 集團旗
下子公司有華夏、台達化、亞聚及越峰。，
2025 EVA 產品銷售佔比分佈：熱熔膠級
27%、太陽能級 41%、發泡級 32%。

主要客戶： 
主要競爭對手：

葉家宏 yili.chen@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

台聚 (1304 TT) 
EVA 新增產能壓力仍大

永豐觀點 
隨著美伊戰爭情緒有所緩解，原油價格回落，塑化產品報價反轉，終端客戶備貨心
態趨於保守， 短期台聚雖有望受惠低價庫存利益，長期則仍須面對中國大量投入的
新增產能壓力，壓抑利差表現，故研究部維持保守看待台聚獲利表現。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議維持中立： 近期因美伊戰爭情緒有所緩解，終端客戶備貨已趨於保守觀
望，EVA 報價反轉下跌，原料乙烯、VAM 仍位居高檔，將造成 EVA 利差持續受
限，而中國2H26 預期仍有超過 100 萬噸 EVA 新增產能將投產，EVA 供給壓力預
期將持續增大，壓抑 EVA 產品利差，故研究部維持台聚中立投資建議。

ESG 評析 
台聚企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。
SinoPac Securities Investment Service licenses and applies the SASB Materiality Map® 
General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

### Page 2

2

台聚為 HDPE、LLDPE、LDPE 及 EVA 生產廠商：台聚成立於 1965 年，為國內第
二大的石化集團，主要生產產品為高密度聚乙烯（ HDPE） ，線型低密度聚乙烯
（LLDPE） ，低密度聚乙烯樹脂 （LDPE Resin） 及乙烯醋酸乙烯酯共聚合樹脂 （EVA 
Resin） ，集團旗下子公司有華夏、台達化、亞聚及越峰。公司主要有兩座廠，一廠
為高壓法 LDPE/ EVA 廠，設備為四套高壓釜式生產線，總產能15 萬噸，二廠為低
壓法 HDPE/ LLDPE 廠 ， 設備為一套氣相法HDPE/ LLDPE 生產線 ， 合計產能13 萬
噸，2025 EVA 產品銷售佔比分佈：熱熔膠級27%、太陽能級 41%、發泡級 32%。 
1Q26 稅後 EPS 為-0.41 元，優於預期：台聚 1Q26 營收 107.47 億元（+8.1% 
QoQ，-11.5% YoY） ， 符合預期 ， 毛利率2.86%（-2.48 pts. QoQ，-0.73 pts. YoY），
低於預期 ， 主要係因原料乙烯於3M25 報價急漲 ， 然台聚漲價訂單多反應於4M25
之後，影響 1Q26 毛利率未如預期，稅後虧損 4.4 億元（虧損縮小 QoQ，虧損擴
大 YoY） ，其中，業外轉投資古雷石化認列一次性地方政府補助款，認列古雷石化
虧損金額較預期低，1Q26 稅後 EPS -0.41 元，優於預期。

營運現況與分析

### Page 3

2Q26 獲利預期承壓：展望 2Q26，中國自 4M26 開始取消光伏出口退稅 13%，
帶動 EVA 行情於 1Q26 止跌反彈，惟市場買氣在漲價後受到壓抑，光伏EVA 需求
明顯轉弱，而市場行情受到美伊戰爭預期緩解，且中國 EVA 供過於求陰影壟罩影
響 ， 下游客戶觀望氣氛濃厚 ， 備貨趨於保守 ， 而台聚受到中油新三輕設備異常影響 ，
4M26 至 5M26 開工率預期降至約 60%，因子公司華夏 2Q26 營收預期將顯著季
增，帶動台聚營收預期仍將季增，且受惠高價訂單出貨及低價庫存利益，本業獲利
預期好轉，惟缺少業外古雷石化一次性補助款，認列虧損金額應將較1Q26 擴大，
壓抑台聚獲利表現，基此，研究部預估 2Q26 營收 115.41 億元（+7.4％QoQ，-
0.3％ YoY） ，預估毛利率8.98%（+7.18 pts. QoQ，+6.12 pts. YoY） ，預估稅後
虧損 5.93 億元（虧損擴大 QoQ，虧損縮小 YoY） ，稅後EPS -0.55 元。

Jason.JC.Tsai@Yuanta.com

charleschen24@yuanta.com

世足賽訂單維繫營運，維持 2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 3.21 元之預估，
年增 28.4% 
聚陽 5 月營收 27.09 億元，月增 3.9%，年增 8.8%，營收達成率 65%，符
合預期，世足賽訂單貢獻維繫營運，維持 2Q26 淡季營收 81.77 億元之預估，
季減 8.1%，年增 6.3%，毛利率維持 23.8%，營業利益 10.77 億元，季減
21.8%，年增 14.4%，稅後 EPS 維持 3.21 元，季減 26.3%，年增 28.4%。 
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 下修 5.6%至 4.09 元，但年增 17.6% 
3Q26 傳統旺季，客戶庫存偏低，回補庫存訂單需求維繫營運，惟美伊和談
進度干擾，品牌傾向短單急單的下單模式，預估出貨量由年增高個位數下修
至年增中個位數，預估 3Q26 營收下修 3%至 93.63 億元，季增 14.5%，年
增 5.3%，毛利率下修 0.9ppt 至 24.7%，營業利益下修 5.5%至 13.51 億元，
季增 25.4%，年增 23.7%，稅後 EPS 下修 5.6%至 4.09 元，季增 27.6%，
年增 17.6%。3Q26 營收及獲利仍維持年增格局，營運尚屬穩健。 
美伊和談變數干擾造成波折，但復甦方向不變，評價偏低 
展望 2026 年，聚陽的品牌客戶庫存陸續去化，回補庫存的訂單需求仍在，
日系客戶訂單穩健，可成長雙位數，來自 Dick’s Sport 訂單亦可年增雙位數，
Under Armour 訂單有機會年增 50%，Gap 旗下運動服飾品牌 Athleta 訂單
恢復增長，維繫營運基本盤，美伊和談變數造成波折，但復甦方向不變，預
估 2026 年營收 346.3 億元，年增 0.6%，稅後 EPS 為 15.45 元，年增 5.3%，
重啟成長，目前本益比 14.6 倍，在 14 至 24 倍歷史本益比區間下緣，評價
偏低，投資價值浮現，重申買進建議，目標價維持 310 元(20 倍 PER X 
2026 年稅後 EPS)。

元大觀點 
⧫  
毛利率優於預期且業外利益貢獻，1Q26 稅後 EPS 為 4.35 元，季增
23.5%，年減 15.5%，優於元大預期 8.8%。世足賽訂單維繫營運，維
持 2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 3.21 元之預估，年增 28.4%。 
⧫  
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 反映 ASP 年減下修 5.6%至 4.09 元，年增
17.6%，估 2026 年稅後 EPS 為 15.45 元，年增 5.5%，恢復正成長。 
⧫  
美伊和談變數干擾造成波折，但復甦方向不變，本益比 14.6 倍，評價
偏低，目標價維持 310 元，基於 20 倍本益比、2026 年 EPS 評價。

### Page 2

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：成衣 2026 年 6 月 3 日 Makalot 第2 頁，共 17頁  
營運分析  
毛利率優於預期且業外利益貢獻，1Q26 稅後 EPS 為 4.35 元，季增 23.5%，年減
15.5%，優於預期 8.8% 
聚陽 1Q26 營收 88.97 億元(原估 90.36 億元)，季增 12.3%，年減 10.4%，低於預期 1.5%，毛利率
僅季減 0.6ppt 至 26.1%(原估 25.3%)，年減 1.7ppt，優於預期，而費用控管好轉，營益率季增
2ppt 至 15.5%(原估 14.7%)，年減 0.4ppt，營業利益 13.77 億元(原估 13.27 億元)，季增 29.1%，
年減 12.8%，優於預期 3.8%，業外匯兌利益貢獻約 2800 萬元，稅後 EPS 為 4.35 元(原估 4.0 元)，
季增 23.5%，年減 15.5%，優於元大預期 8.8%。生產效率提升及產品組合較佳，帶動毛利率優於
預期。

世足賽訂單維繫營運，彌補戰爭變數干擾，維持 2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 3.21 元之預估，
年增 28.4% 
聚陽公布 2026 年 5 月營收 27.09 億元，月增 3.9%，年增 8.8%，以原預估 2Q26 營收 81.77 億元
(季減 8.1%，年增 6.3%)計算，營收達成率 65%，符合預期，營收月增，乃 4 月為季節交替傳統淡
季，5 月出貨逐漸轉旺。其中 5 月出貨量年增 6%多，匯率有利約 2%多，ASP 則年持平。來自世
足賽訂單貢獻維繫營運，彌補美伊戰爭變數干擾，維持 2Q26 淡季營收 81.77 億元之預估，季減
8.1%，年增 6.3%，毛利率維持 23.8%，營益率維持 13.2%，營業利益維持 10.77 億元，季減
21.8%，年增 14.4%，稅後 EPS 維持 3.21 元，季減 26.3%，年增 28.4%。2Q26 營收及獲利維持
年增格局，營運穩健。

美伊和談變數干擾，預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 下修 5.6%至 4.09 元，但年增 17.6% 
展望 3Q26 傳統旺季，客戶庫存偏低，預期回補庫存訂單需求維繫營運，惟最大變數仍係美伊 和談
進度干擾，油價維持高檔，引發通膨升溫排擠消費之擔憂，造成市場疑慮，品牌下單略顯猶豫，傾
向短單急單的下單模式，能見度不高，來自品牌客戶整體出貨規劃作調整幅度約 3%，預估出貨量
由年增高個位數下修至年增中個位數，而台幣匯率較 2025 年同期回貶，彌補 ASP 年減 3%之拖累，
預估 3Q26 營收下修 3%至 93.63 億元(原估 96.49 億元)，季增 14.5%，年增 5.3%，毛利率下修
0.9ppt 至 24.7%(原估 25.6%)，營益率下修 0.4ppt 至 14.4%(原估 14.8%)，營業利益下修 5.5%至
13.51 億元(原估 14.3 億元)，季增 25.4%，年增 23.7%，稅後 EPS 下修 5.6%至 4.09 元(原估 4.33
元)，季增 27.6%，年增 17.6%。3Q26 毛利率下修，係反映 ASP 年減，削弱部分獲利動能，不過
若客戶有急單挹注，聚陽仍有產能可因應，進而有營收及毛利率上修機會。 3Q26 營收雖下修，但
營收及獲利仍維持年增格局，營運尚屬穩健。

望，不過品牌客戶庫存陸續去化，回補庫存的訂單需求仍在，只是尚待觀察戰爭發展，壓抑短期下
單意願，惟聚陽日系客戶訂單穩健，可成長雙位數，來自 Dick’s Sport 訂單亦可年增雙位數，

年增 8.2%，稅後 EPS 上修 0.4%至 15.45 元(原估 15.39 元)，年增 5.3%。2026 年稅後 EPS 略為
上修係反映 1Q26 獲利優於預期。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：成衣 2026 年 6 月 3 日 Makalot 第5 頁，共 17頁  
產業概況  
美國庫存偏低，回補庫存需求，有利 2026 年營運復甦 
近期美伊戰爭造成油價大漲，推升通膨預期，市場對於排擠消費的擔憂再起，而川普總統打壓油價
及抑制通膨，以推進降息的立場不變，且近期美伊和談進展，油價回落，舒緩通膨擔憂，後續須持
續關注美伊和談發展。我們對於 2026 年看法不悲觀，仍有一些兩個因素可以期待，其一為美國可
望啟動回補庫存，其二 2026 年世足賽可望誘發新的需求，提振 2026 年營運，以下個別分析這兩
因素。 
首先觀察美國服飾零售銷售表現，2026 年 4 月服飾零售銷售額 275.34 億美元，月減 1.5%，年增
5.8%，較前月年增 7.6%放緩，惟連 20 個月 YOY 正成長，銷售表現仍屬穩健。另一方面，美國服
飾庫存則相對平穩，2026 年 3 月服飾零售庫存略增至 584.58 億美元，月增 0.1%，年增 0.2%，庫
存動能低於 1H25 之 2-3%的年增表現。至於服飾存貨週轉天數增加 0.9 天至 63.7 天，仍低於 2025
年初 68-69 天，以及 2023 至 2024 年的 70 天水準，整體庫存偏低。搭配觀察美國紡織品進口
2025 年陷入衰退，而 2025 年年終消費旺季，服飾銷售維持熱絡的動能，將持續去化服飾庫存，預
期 2026 年回補庫存的機率大幅提升。 
圖 4：美國服飾零售銷售趨勢圖

資料來源：Bloomberg、元大投顧整理

圖 5：美國服飾庫存及天數趨勢圖

世足賽商機擴大，聚焦美國再次舉辦且擴大參賽隊伍之效益 
另一方面在於 2026 年最重要的運動賽事焦點就是第 23 屆世界盃足球賽，此次世足賽兩大特點，
美國再次舉辦世足賽以及第三次擴大參賽隊伍，預期賽事熱度會大幅擴散。  
美國相隔 32 年再次舉辦，且是北美三國美加墨共同舉辦，盛況空前。1994 年美國第一次舉辦時，
現場觀賽人口總數由前一屆義大利舉辦的 251.6 萬人增加 42.6%至 358.8 萬人，創歷史新高，後來
各屆賽事都沒有突破這數字。全球電視收視人口總數由前一屆 10.3 億人倍增至 21 億人，年增
103.9%，FIFA 評估世足賽整體商機規模由 3.4 億美元增至 5 億美元，增加 47.1%，美國第一次舉
辦世足賽的效益相當顯著。 
圖 6：歷屆世足賽現場觀賽人口總數趨勢圖

資料來源：FIFA、元大投顧整理

### Page 7

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：成衣 2026 年 6 月 3 日 Makalot 第7 頁，共 17頁  
圖 7：歷屆世足賽電視收視人口總數趨勢圖

資料來源：FIFA、元大投顧整理

2026 年將是第三次擴大參賽隊伍。第一次是 1982 年由 16 支擴大至 24 支，比賽場次由 38 場增至
52 場，現場觀賽人口總數由 154.5 萬人增至 211 萬人，增加 36.6%，電視人口由 6 億人增至 7.5
億人，增加 25%，FIFA 評估的世足賽商機規模由 8 千萬美元增至 1.5 億美元，增加 87.5%。第二
次則是 1998 年由 24 支擴大至 32 支，比賽場次由 52 場增至 64 場，不過現場觀賽人口總數由
358.8 萬人降至 278.5 萬人，年減 22.4%，較不如預期，而電視人口則由 21 億人增至 28.8 億人，
年增 37.1%，FIFA 評估世足賽商機規模由 5 億美元增至 15.6 億美元，增加 212%。

圖 8：世足賽商機規模趨勢圖

資料來源：FIFA、元大投顧整理

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：成衣 2026 年 6 月 3 日 Makalot 第8 頁，共 17頁  
圖 9：歷屆世足賽參賽隊伍擴大的相關資訊彙整表

資料來源：FIFA、元大投顧整理

展望 2026 年世足賽，將是第三次擴大參賽隊伍，由 32 支擴大至 48 支，比賽場次會由 64 場暴增
至 104 場，比賽期間也會拉長，世足賽風潮將更熱烈，保守預估現場觀賽人口來到 550 萬人，年
增 61.6%，樂觀版應有機會突破 650 萬人。電視人口則可來到 80 億人，年增 60%，FIFA 預估商
機規模擴大至 110 億美元，年增 45.3%。預期在世足賽觀賽人口增加，商機擴大的帶動，對於足球
鞋及運動鞋，與足球相關服飾的銷售均有提振作用，提振成衣及製鞋族群於 2026 年重啟成長軌道，
仍可正向期待。

### Page 9

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：成衣 2026 年 6 月 3 日 Makalot 第9 頁，共 17頁  
獲利調整與股票評價  
復甦略有波折，但復甦方向未變，2026 年重啟成長，評價偏低 
展望 2026 年，美伊和談進度不確定性恐影響 2H26 美國消費動能以及聚陽來自美系客戶的訂單展
望，不過品牌客戶庫存陸續去化，回補庫存的訂單需求仍在，只是尚待觀察戰爭發展，壓抑短期下
單意願，惟聚陽日系客戶訂單穩健，可成長雙位數，來自 Dick’s Sport 訂單亦可年增雙位數，
Under Armour 訂單有機會年增 50%，Gap 旗下運動服飾品牌 Athleta 訂單恢復增長，運動服飾訂
單需求維繫營運基本盤，美伊和談進度不確定性造成訂單復甦略有波折，但復甦方向沒有改變，預
估 2026 年營收下修 1.5%至 346.3 億元(原估 351.61 億元)，年增 0.6%，毛利率維持 25.2%之預估，
營益率略為上修 0.1ppt 至 14.6%(原估 14.5%)，營業利益下修 0.9%至 50.65 億元(原估 51.10 億元)，
年增 8.2%，稅後 EPS 上修 0.4%至 15.45 元(原估 15.39 元)，年增 5.3%。2026 年稅後 EPS 略為
上修係反映 1Q26 獲利優於預期。 
美伊和談的不確定性干擾聚陽來自美系客戶的訂單展望，惟考量 :1) 5 月營收 27.09 億元，月增
3.9%，年增 8.8%，動能穩健。2) 預估 2Q26 營收 81.77 億元，年增 6.3%，稅後 EPS 為 3.21 元，
年增 28.4%，維持年增格局。3) 預估 3Q26 營收 93.63 億元，年增 5.3%，稅後 EPS 為 4.09 元，
年增 17.6%，延續復甦。4) 戰爭造成訂單復甦略有波折，但復甦方向沒有改變，預估 2026 年營收
346.3 億元，年增 0.6%，稅後 EPS 為 15.45 元，年增 5.3%，重啟成長，目前本益比約 14.6 倍，
在 14 至 24 倍歷史本益比區間的下緣，評價偏低，投資價值浮現，重申買進建議，目標價維持 310
元(20 倍 PER X 2026 年稅後 EPS)。

公司簡介  
公司成立於 1990 年 1 月，是亞太地區知名的成衣製造商，主要業務為成衣的設計、製造及銷售，其客戶為全球及區域型之
大型成衣零售商，是全球第五大成衣代工商。 2015 年公司切入機能性服飾領域，運動服飾佔營收比重逐年提升。公司五大
廠區包括印尼、柬埔寨、越南、菲律賓、中國大陸，採「台灣接單，全球生產」的營運模式。公司成衣商品主要銷售地區
為美國市場，主要客戶包含百貨公司 (Kohl's JC Penney) 、大型賣場(Target、Wal-mart)、成衣專賣店 (Gap、Old Navy 、
Hanesbrands、Carter's、MANGO、Under Armour 等)；歐洲市場客戶有 ZARA、H&M 等，亞洲市場客戶包括台灣網購品
牌 Lativ、日商迅銷集團等。

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 聚陽整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於領先的位置，
在奢華服飾行業中的公司中排名領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 聚陽的整體曝險屬於低等水準，略低於紡織品行業平均水準。紡織業中的公司通常較需要改善
的 ESG 議題包含人力資本、商業道德和人權供應鏈等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：聚陽在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力為平均水準。公司採取了一些措施來
管理與重大 ESG 問題相關的風險，但這些措施不足以管理其所有風險。儘管如此，該公司尚未捲入任何具有重大財務
影響的風險事件。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

QoQ+12.81% ， 預估營業利益 10.96 億元 ，YoY+31.29% ，較 1Q26
由虧轉盈，預估稅後盈餘 6.00 億元， YoY+436.25% ，
QoQ+2409% ，預估稅後 EPS 1.98 元。

三 . 預估 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元，
YoY+15.98%  
商用市場 1Q26 營收金額 2.35 億美元， YoY+17.21% 。商用市場
2026 年營運成長動能包括 ： 1， 在歐美健身房主要客戶滲透率持續
提升，另外也拓展歐美各國本土前五大健身房客戶。 2，產品線擴
展，增加高階健身器材產品線，以便拓展高端健身房市場，增加新
客群。 3，商用健身器材零組件主要以台灣與中國上海為主，預期
在越南工廠生產比重提升帶動下，將有助整體毛利率提升。 預估喬
山 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元， YoY+15.98% 。

四. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 13.00 元 
喬山 2026 年在歐美健身房客戶持續在海外設據點 ， 加上客戶轉單
效益 ， 搭配旗下越南廠生產效率提升與產能擴充 ， 將有助喬山 2026
年營運持續成長。宏遠預估喬山 2026 年營收金額 630.00 億元，
YoY+15.96% ，預估營業利益 59.86 億元， YoY+66.66% ，預估稅
後盈餘 39.44 億元， YoY+43.43% ，預估稅後 EPS 13.00 元。

五. 投資建議買進，目標價 156 元 
觀察喬山歷史 PER 落在 8~33X，2026 年 ROE 26.7%，PER 9.1X，
評價在低檔水準，加上 2026 年營運成長，故投資建議買進，目標
價 156 元。 (12X 2026 年稅後 EPS)

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
喬山健康科技創立於 1975 年，資本額 30.34 億元，並於 2003 年
掛牌上市，喬山為亞洲第一、世界前三大的國際專業運動健身器
材集團公司，產品包括電動跑步機、室內健身車、橢圓訓練機、
重量訓練器材、划船器材、電動按摩椅、心肺訓練器材等。喬山
以「健康、價值、共享」為企業經營理念，專注於健康科技事業
的發展，並以 Matrix 、Vision 和 Horizon 自有品牌行銷全世界 80
餘國。生產基地位於台中、上海、與美國越南，其中台中生產高
單價的商用產品、上海主要生產製造量大的家用產品與小部分商
用產品、美國主要生產製造特殊重量訓練產品、越南主要生產製
造家用產品為主。

二.品牌定位與銷售通路  
喬山旗下擁有 Matrix 、Vision 和 Horizon 三項自有品牌，其中
Martrix 主打高價位商用市場 ， 其主要銷售對象包括健身房 、 飯店、
運動員訓練中心、大型公司、運動專賣店等； Vision 主打中高價
位的市場，主要銷售對象以大型公司、運動專賣店為主； Horizon
主打家用市場為主，銷售對象包括運動專賣店、電商與大型運動
器材專賣店。

三. 2026 年營運概況及展望

全球健身器材市場 100 億美元 (宏遠投顧推估 )，其中商用市場 50
億美元、家用市場 50 億美元。觀察全球主要廠商 2024 年營收金
額概況： Life 6.75 億美元， YoY+4.2%( 宏遠投顧推估 )；TG 7.67
億美元 ，YoY+13.6%；喬 山10.39 億美元 ，YoY+18.3%；Precor2.61
億美元， YoY+26.1% ；Pelonton 27.0 億美元， YoY-1.1% 。

預估 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元， YoY+15.98%

喬山商用市場客戶包括 ： 健身房北美星球健身 、 歐洲 BASIC-FIT 、
拉丁美洲 Smart Fit 以及全球連鎖性健身房 Anytime Finess 、台 灣
World GYM 與健身工廠等；飯店客戶 Marriott 、STARWOOD 、
FOUR SEASONS 等。

圖五、喬山健身房客戶

資料來源：喬山 &宏遠投顧彙整

商用市場 2025 年營收金額 12.33 億美元， YoY+18.67% 。觀察喬
山商用市場 2025 年營運成長動能包括： 1，在中國大陸健身器材
出口至美國課徵高額關稅 ， 相對於台灣與越南關稅較低，帶動健身
房客戶訂單轉向台灣與越南地區。 2，歐洲大型客戶持續拓展美國
市場，另外美國主要客戶也拓展歐洲市場，歐洲與美國主要客戶
2025 年分別成長 25~28% 與 21~24% 。3，歐洲地區受惠中小型客
戶需求增溫，帶動歐洲地成長 25~30% 。

2026 年營運成長動能包括 ： 1， 在歐美健身房主要客戶滲透率持續
提升，另外也拓展歐美各國本土前五大健身房客戶。 2，產品線擴
展，增加高階健身器材產品線，以便拓展高端健身房市場，增加新
客群。 3，商用健身器材零組件主要以台灣與中國上海為主，預期
在越南工廠生產比重提升帶動下，將有助整體毛利率提升。 預估喬
山 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元， YoY+15.98% 。

圖六、擴展商用市場新產品 /新通路

資料來源：喬山 &宏遠投顧彙整

預估 2026 年家用市場營收金額 4.00 億美元， YoY+12.68%

喬山家用市場主要以運動專賣店、電商與大型運動器材專賣店為
主。喬山為拓展家用市場零售通路，陸續在 2015 年~2016 年購併
2nd Wind 、BUSY BODY 、Leisure Fitness 與 Gym Source 等健
身器材銷售通路，喬山在北美銷售通路據點共 103 家，據點數占
北美 30% 。

家用市場 2025 年營收金額 3.55 億美元 ，YoY+17.16% 。喬 山2025
年家用營運成長主要包括： 1，BowFlex 在 2025 年貢獻營收金額
1.1~1.2 億美元， YoY+53~67% 。2，觀察歐洲地區與拉丁美洲受
惠家用健身器材營運基期較低，商用健身房興起帶動家用市場需
求熱絡，帶動歐洲地區與拉丁美洲家用市場分別成長 45~48% 與
22~25% 。3，觀察全球主要家用健身器材廠商，生產基地集中在
中國大陸，在美國對中國大陸出口產品課徵高額關稅因素，許多
訂單己移至越南生產，帶動喬山越南廠稼動率提升。

家用市場 1Q26 營收金額 1.01 億美元， YoY+12.85% 。家用市場
2026 年營運成長動能包括： 1，家用健身器材經歷 2020~2021 年
上半年疫情期間大好， 2021 下半年至 2023 年庫存調整， 2025 年
因關稅訂單偏保守，觀察零售通路庫存已達相對低檔水準，加上
己有廠商退出市場，將有助家用市場成長。 2，喬山持續拓展零售
據點效益顯現，例如大型量販店己向喬山採購多項家用產品線，
帶動大型量販通路營收。 3，家用旗下 BowFlex 在 2026 年推出新
產品，搶攻北美家用市場，將有助 BowFlex  2026 年出貨成長。
綜合上述，預估 2025 年喬山家用市場營收金額 4.00 億美元，
YoY+12.68% 。

預估富士醫療 (含自有品牌按摩椅 )貢獻 2026 年營收金額 1.70 億
美元， YoY+6.9%

喬山 10/29/2019 公告，以 67 億日幣 （約當台幣 20 億元），向「朝
日生活與健康股份有限公司 Asahi Life & Health Corporation 」買
下子公司「富士醫療器股份有限公司 FUJI MEDICAL 
INSTRUMENT 」 60% 股權，「富士醫療器股份有限公司 FUJI 
MEDICAL INSTRUMENT 」為日本最大按摩椅製造商，擁有業界
領先產品開發和品牌實力，另外在日本佈有綿密的銷售通路，觀
察富士醫療器年營收金額 180~200 億日幣 (約當美元 1.8~2 億美
元)，己於 03/2020 完成股權交割。喬山 12/09/2022 公告取得富
士醫療器 40% 股權 ， 交易完成後喬山持有富士醫療器 100% 股權。

摩椅 2025 年營收金額 0.31 億美元， YoY+6.9% 。觀察富士醫療營
收成長主因在於富士醫療拓展日本以外的外銷市場有成。

YoY+9.09% 預期 2026 年，在富士醫療持續拓展日本以外的外銷
市場，加上喬山自有品牌按摩椅穩定成長。預估富士醫療 2026 年

2026 年營收金額 0.34 億元 ，YoY+9.68%， 合計兩者貢獻喬山 2026
年營收金額 1.70 億美元， YoY+6.9% 。

公告配發現金股利 4.5 元，現金殖利率 3.8%

利政策，配發現金股利 4 元，股利發放率 50% 。喬山公告 2025
年股利政策，將配發現金股利 4.5 元，現金殖利率 3.8% 。

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關
條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保證。非經本公司同意，不得
將本報告加以引用或轉載。 
1 
2603 長榮 ( EMC )

報告日 期： 2026 / 05 / 29 研 究 員 ： 林 奕 頎(Yichi Lin)  
壹、投 資 建 議 
一.美 伊 各 懷 鬼 胎 ， 拉 扯 協 議 ，以 拖 待 變 ，油價 及 航 商 獲 得 喘 息 
2 月 28 日 美 伊 戰 爭 爆 發 ， 海 運 燃 油 （ 新 加 坡 ） 從2 月 27 日每
噸 697 美 元 ， 狂 飆 至4 月 2 日每噸  1,789 美元， 上漲 157% 。 
後 續 ， 美 伊 進 行 停 火 協 商 ， 原 油 市 場 恐 慌 降 溫 ， 油 價 回 跌 。 
5 月 26 日 最 新 報 價， 海 運 燃 油 每 噸 1,048 美 元， 較 美 伊 戰 爭 爆
發 前 的 2 月 27 日 697 美 元 ，上漲 50% 。 
同 期 間，航 商 因 應市場 恐慌（ 擔 心 經 濟 衰 退 ），進 行 大 幅 減 班 ，
更 為 轉 嫁海 運 燃 油成 本 ， 大 幅 調 漲 運 價 ，SCFI 運 價 指 數 ， 從2
月 27 日 1,333 .11 一 路 走 升 至5 月 28 日 2,218.15 ，上漲 66%。 
油價暴漲，讓貨櫃航商驚慌，緊緊抱團，落實減班，大幅推升
運價， 超 額 轉 嫁 海 運 燃 油 成 本， 反 成 為 美 伊 戰 爭 受 惠 者。 
二.估 2Q / 2026 稅後 EPS + 5. 97 元， YoY + 18%，QoQ + 56%  
1Q / 202 6 單季 SCFI 運 價 指 數 平 均 1,491 。 
2Q / 2026 單季 SCFI 運 價 指 數 （ 至5 月 28 日 ） 平 均 1,961 。 
1Q / 202 6 單 季 海 運 燃 油 平 均 每 噸 840 美元 。 
2Q / 2026 單 季 海 運 燃 油（ 至5 月 26 日 ）平 均 每噸 1,238 美元 。 
附 註 ：5 月 28 日 SCFI 指數  2,218； 26 日 海 運 燃 油 1,048 美元。 
宏 遠 預 期，受 惠 季 節 性 轉 強 及 超 額 轉 嫁 燃 油，估計 長榮 2Q / 
2026 單 季 營 收QoQ + 152 億 元； 燃 油 支 出QoQ + 60 億 元； 推
升營業利益 QoQ + 74 億元 ； 單 季 稅 後EPS + 5.97 元 ， 優 於1Q 
/ 2026 的 + 3.84 元 ， 亦 優 於2Q / 2025 同 期 的 + 5.06 元。  
三.短 期 間 不 易 出 現 大 規 模 軍 事 衝 突 ，市 場 信 心 恢 復 ， 壓 抑 油 價 
伊 朗 面 對 經濟大幅衰退  及 石油停產 （ 毀 壞 油 田 ）的 壓 力 。 
美 國 面 對 戰爭權力決議  60 天限制  及 11 月 3 日 期 中 大 選。 
《 戰 爭 權 力 決 議 》 規 定 ， 若 總 統 未 獲 國 會 授 權 派 兵 作 戰 ，
必 須 在 60 天 內 向 國 會 請 求 授 權。 川普 3 月 2 日 向 國 會 報
告 ， 起 算60 天 ； 宣 稱4 月 7 日 已 停 戰 ， 無 須 國 會 授 權 。 
美伊雙方各有壓力，都需喘息空間，因此，雙方以拖待變的心
態濃厚，短期間，不易爆發大規模軍事衝擊，有利於恢復市場
信 心 ， 壓 抑 短 期 間 油 價 漲 勢 （長 期 則 必 須 觀 察 庫 存 水 準）。 
四.買進 ：季 節 性 轉 強 ； 美伊以拖待變 ； 2Q 及 3Q 獲 利 逐 季 走 高 
貨 櫃 航商， 面對 恐 慌 ， 緊 緊 抱 團 ， 大 幅 拉 高 運 價 ， 超 額 轉 嫁 燃
油 成 本 ； 同 時 ， 季 節 性 轉 強 ，即將進入 3Q 旺季；  此 外 ， 美
伊 雙 方 以 拖 待 變，有 利 恢 復 市 場 信 心，油 價 恐 慌短期 退 燒； 宏
遠 預 期 貨櫃航商 2Q 及 3Q 獲 利 逐 季 走 高； 建 議 買 進 長 榮。
投資評等：買進（調升） 
目標價位：258 元 
0.90 X 2026 年底每股淨值

參考價位：211.0 元 
 P/B：0.74 X 2026 年底每股淨值 
   2026 / 05 / 28 收盤

2026 年稅後 EPS 預估值 
從 + 9.86 元 調升至 + 21.00 元

股價利多： 
(1) 現金股利 16 元，現金殖利率
超過 7%，有利支撐股價。 
(2) 運價上漲， 超額轉嫁燃油成
本，有利於推升航商獲利。 
(3) 美伊各有壓力，以拖待變，油
價恐慌短期退燒。 
(4) 貨櫃航運季節性轉強， 即將進
入 3Q 旺季 ， 有利於支撐運價 。

貳、公 司 概 況 
一.公司簡介  
長榮 ， 以 遠 洋 貨 櫃 航 線（ 美 西 線 ； 美 東 線 ； 歐 洲 線 ）， 為 主 。

目 前 船 隊 （截至 202 6 年 5 月 28 日 ； 依 據 Alphaline r 統 計 資 料）： 
總營運 艘數 240 艘，總營運 TEU 數為 1,989,787  TEU （ 佔 全 球 貨 櫃
船 隊 運 力 的5.9%）。 
自 有 ：165 艘 ， 總 TEU 數 1,406 ,131  TEU 。 
租 賃 ： 75 艘，總 TEU 數  583 ,656  TEU 。

長榮 造 船 計 畫：待交船  74 艘， 合 計 891 ,972  TEU ，約 當長 榮 目 前
船隊 運 力 的 44.8 % 。

長 榮 船 隊 運 力 （ 依 據202 6 年 4 月 24 日 法 說資 料 ） ： 
2025 年 底 ：1,9 56,233  TEU 。 
2026 年 底 ：2,0 71,289  TEU ，YoY + 6% 。 
202 7 年 底 ：2,408 ,593  TEU ，YoY + 1 6%。

二.營運概況 及展望  
1. 美 伊 戰 爭 爆 發 ，航 商 抱 團 拉 抬 運 價 ， 超 額 轉 嫁海 運 燃 油成本  
長 榮 海 運1Q / 2026 ， 海 運 燃 油佔 合 併 營 業 成 本19% 。 
依照 1Q / 2026 的 成 本 結 構，假 設 其 他 條 件 不 變 ， 則 平均油價  每
上漲  10%， 平 均 運 價 僅 需 上 漲 2%， 即 可超額 轉 嫁 燃 油 上 漲 成 本。

1Q / 2026 單 季 海 運 燃 油 平 均 每 噸 840 美 元 。 
2Q / 2026 單 季 海 運 燃 油 （至 5 月 26 日 ） 平 均 每 噸 1,238 美元。  
2Q / 2026 ，單季海運燃油 QoQ + 47% 。

1Q / 2026 單季 SCFI 運 價 指 數 平 均 1,491 。 
2Q / 2026 單季 SCFI 運 價 指 數 （至 5 月 28 日 ）平均  1,961 。 
2Q / 2026 單季 SCFI 運 價 指 數 QoQ + 32% 。

長榮是全球第 7 大貨櫃航商

(1) 季節性轉強，需求上升。 
(2) 產業恐慌，落實減班，大
幅拉升運價。 
(3) 美伊雙方以拖待變，油價
恐慌短期退燒。

貨櫃航商超額轉嫁燃油成本 
預期將推升 2Q 及 3Q 獲利

(1)4Q 貨櫃航運季節性轉弱 
(2)11 月美國期中選舉結束 
貨櫃航運可能再度大幅下滑

3

在 10% 油 價 漲 幅，僅 需不到  2% 運 價 漲 幅 即 可 轉 嫁 的 情 況 下 ： 
航 商 拉 抬2Q / 2026 單季 SCFI 運 價 指 數QoQ + 32% ， 即 使 假 設 半
數 為 固 定 價 格 的 長 約 ， 使 得整體 運價 （現貨  + 長 約 ）實 際 漲 幅
縮 減 為 16% ，這 亦 足 夠 轉 嫁 超 過 80% 的 燃 油 上 漲 成 本； 但 2Q / 
2026 海 運 燃 油 價 格QoQ + 47% ； 因 此 ， 可 以 合 理 推 估 ， 貨 櫃 航
商 已 超 額 轉 嫁 燃 油 上 漲 成 本，貨 櫃 航 商2Q 獲 利 將 明 顯 優 於1Q 。

此 外 ， 依 據 最 新 報 價 ， 貨 櫃 航 商 超 額 轉 嫁 的 情 況 更 為 嚴 重： 
5 月 28 日 SCFI 指數  2,218  ； 高 於 2Q / 2026 單 季 平 均 1,961 。 
5 月 26 日 海 運 燃 油 1,048  ； 低 於 2Q / 2026 單 季 平 均1,238 。

最 新 資 料，顯 示，運 價 持 續 上 漲，海 運 燃 油 持 續 下 跌，貨 櫃 航 商
超 額 轉 嫁 的 幅 度 不 斷 擴 大； 因 此 ， 可 以 合 理 預 期 貨 櫃 航 商2Q / 
2026 獲 利 將 明 顯 優 於1Q / 2026 。

1.估 2026 年稅後 EPS + 21 元 
宏 遠 估 計 ， 長 榮2026 年：  
營收  3,847 .82 億元， YoY + 2%； 
長榮 公告 2026 年 1Q 財報 營收 YoY – 21%。 
長 榮 公 告2026 年 4 月 單 月 營 收YoY + 5% 。

營 業 利 益 526.55 億 元 ，YoY - 29%； 
(1) 預期 2026 年 海 運 燃 油 平 均 價 格 上 漲 超 過 7 成。 
(2) 航 商 抱 團 減 班 ， 拉 高運價 ， 轉 嫁 燃 油 上 漲 成 本 。 
即 使 運 價 拉 升 ， 但2026 年 燃 油 成 本 依 然 大 幅 高 於2025 年，
造成 2026 年 營 業 利 益較 2025 年 明 顯滑 落 。

長榮公告 1Q / 2026 財 報 營 業 利 益 89.48 億 元 ，YoY – 69%。

業 外 淨 收 支 + 39.08 億元， YoY - 55%； 
(1) 連年 發放 高額 現 金 股 利 ， 造 成 利 息 收 入 減 少 。 
長榮 1Q / 2026 財報  利 息 收 入 16.74 億 元 ，YoY – 33%； 
2026 年 預 計 發 放2025 年 每 股 現 金 股 利 + 16 元 ， 將 使
2026 年 2H 利 息 收 入 低 於1H 。 
(2) 受 景氣  供 需 、油價 高漲  影 響 ，2026 年 權 益 法 轉 投 資
收 益 貢 獻減少。  
(3) 購 買 取 得 新 船 ，2026 年 利 息 費用 上 升 。

## Document 069: 260528_ms_GUC-DG

Source PDF: all report pdf/260528_ms_GUC-DG.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-05-28
Pages: 19
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

M
Idea
1) Tesla AI chip  market share gets diluted by Samsung foundry: GUC stock fell
10% today (vs. Taiex -2%). Investors are concerned about potential competition/
share loss for GUC's existing projects/customers. Given leading-edge capacity
shortages and higher TSMC wafer costs, our recent industry checks show that the
AI5 packaging configuration could shift from two AI5 chips to one AI5 plus a co-
packaged backup AI4 built on Samsung 5nm. What’s more, both Samsung and Intel

foundries are likely to produce next-gen 2nm AI6 as we noted in our previous
Foundry Floorplan
. Although we have not yet baked any AI6 revenue into our
current GUC forecast, we think it might disappoint some bullish investors who
expect GUC to handle the majority of the next-gen Tesla AI project.
2) Google CPU projects could be shared by Samsung foundry as well:  Google may
also seek other foundry suppliers or potentially other ASIC partners for more

capacity. According to our industry checks,
Samsung's foundry is also likely to
produce next-gen Google CPU (Axion 3) from its SF2 (or 2nm) process, with yields
gradually improving.  We see potential market share loss for GUC’s major CPU
business in the coming years, offsetting Google CPU demand strength at GUC. We
believe we have baked some conservatism into contribution from GUC’s major
projects — including Google CPU (2mn units in 2026 and 2.5mn in 2027 and Tesla

AI5 (800k units in 2027 and 1.5mn in 2028 — (without AI6). Therefore, we make no
changes to our earnings forecasts. We believe potential competition should have
limited near-term impact. However, we don't see upside to our current earnings
forecasts or price target of NT$4,888 either.
Move GUC to EW to take profits until we see new project wins:  With the medium-
/long-term outlook affected and after the share price rally over the past two
months (+135% vs. Taiex +33%), we think it’s time to take profits, with P/E now at

50x our 2027 EPS. We could turn constructive again if 1) TSMC can allocate more
wafers to GUC with price discounts to regain market share, and/or 2) GUC wins new
major CSP projects.

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Still strong chip demand from CPU  and AI business
How GUC  could participate in  TSMC's   automotive chip
foundry service
In TSMC: Thoughts about auto semi foundry dynamics; OW (1 Aug 2025), we noted that
the new AI5 will be manufactured using TSMC's 3nm process node and is expected to
deliver 4-5x higher performance than its predecessor, AI4. GUC could help in the design by
working closely with TSMC. We believe the Tesla AI project will only get bigger when the

new generation chip is introduced. For the  next-generation Tesla chip, despite the
competition from other foundries,  we still expect GUC to get involved in TSMC's version
of the AI chip too.
In the same report, we also noted that TSMC is the major autonomous driving chip
foundry for merchant chip suppliers such as Horizon Robotics and Nvidia (e.g., the Thor
chip).   We believe some of those merchant chips may go through a design service to ensure

time to market.
Some Chinese EV OEMs with custom ASIC chip designs are also potential customers for
Asian ASIC service providers and TSMC. For example, Alchip (OW) is starting the second
5nm ASIC for Li Auto at TSMC. XPeng is also seeking design service for its new generation
ASIC.
Exhibit 1: Tesla AI5
Source: Tesla
Strong Arm-based CPU revenue to dilute gross margin, but
still be positive for GUC's EPS

### Page 3

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 3
CPU turnkey revenue carries a low gross margin – likely in the single digits – because this
is a “Turnkey 2 or 3” project, where GUC provides advanced packaging design support (e.g.,
silicon interposers and ABF substrates) but does not own the photomasks for the ASIC
designs. Our checks  suggest that   Google CPU gross margin could also be 2-3ppts better
than our previous anticipation of 5% given the better order scale at TSMC. We now expect

strong Google CPU demand and already saw Axion CPU demand at least 7mn units from
2026-2028, generating close to US$5bn in revenue (ASP US$700) for GUC over the same
period.
GUC also helps with the Cobalt 200 CPU for Microsoft, an Arm-based design tailored for
Microsoft Cloud. Our checks also indicate strong demand for Cobalt at  >1mn units in 2027
and onwards. We therefore forecast US$500-600mn revenue contribution from this in
2027-2028.

Source: Company data, Morgan Stanley Research estimates
Other major projects in 2026/2027
GUC has indicated that it will have three AI-related projects with US cloud service
providers (CSPs) active in 2026. We believe these CSP projects could account for over
50% of 2026e revenue and look for crypto-related momentum to continue at least

through 1H26, as the company has already secured foundry capacity support. In our view,
this underscores the importance of strong foundry relationships in obtaining scarce
leading-edge capacity. Including GUC’s other AI start-up engagements and IP licensing, we
estimate AI-related revenue will exceed 50% of total revenue in both 2026 and 2027.
• Microsoft: In our previous report, we indicated that GUC supports a Microsoft
3nm AI accelerator (Maia 200) TK2. Our industry checks suggest that Maia 200

tape-out should come in May 2026, with mass production later next year. GUC also
helps on the Cobalt 200 CPU, Arm-based design tailored for Microsoft Cloud.
• Amazon AI inference chips: GUC is helping AWS build the inference chip for AI
speakers, which could also start revenue contribution in 2026 and reach around
US$100mn in 2027.
• SanDisk and Kioxia SSD controllers:  From our industry checks, GUC has also been
working  with SanDisk on  SSD controllers, which should start to contribute some

NRE revenue in 2026, and then ~US$250mn in turnkey revenue in 2027.
• Chinese ADAS chipmaker:  In addition, GUC's auto customers are not limited to
Tesla only. A Chinese ADAS chipmaker  could also contribute revenue  by 2026 and
ramp in 2027.
• GLink IP licensing: In 2026, we also expect GUC to keep licensing its die-to-die
GLink IP to Google’s CPU project, which we estimate could generate US$3-5mn in
annualized revenue.

Relative valuation
GUC's share price has risen nearly 135% since April 2025 given the strong demand from
Google CPU and Tesla AI5 (the TAIEX is up 33% over the same period).
Our price target of NT$4,888 implies 48x our 2027 EPS estimate, lower than +1 SD from

GUC’s historical NTM P/E since 2013. We maintain our view that the design service market
will proliferate with AI ASIC outgrowing AI GPU in the long term. However, we think it’s
time  to take profits, with the stock now trading at 50x our 2027 EPS estimate with
potential competition.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
TSMC (2330.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of 1.2, risk-
free rate of 2.0% and risk premium of 6.0%), an intermediate growth rate of 10.5%, and a
terminal growth rate of 4.0%.
Risks to Upside
n TSMC charges large customers more to keep its gross margin above 56% in the long
term.
n AI semi demand grows more significantly than expected, while TSMC maintains high

market share in leading-edge foundry business.
n Outsourcing from Intel CPU increases in 2025-27.
Risks to Downside
n Inventory correction occurs in 2026.
n Demand for leading edge technologies weakens.
n Costs of overseas fabs grow significantly.
Alchip Technologies Ltd (3661.TW)
Base case value, derived from a residual income model. We assume the cost of equity is con-
stant at 10.4% (2% risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.4 beta). Our intermediate growth rate and

terminal growth rates are 16% and 5%, respectively.
Risks to Upside
n Demand for Chinese GPUs and AI chipsets takes off earlier than expected.
n NRE demand accelerates.
n Alchip wins more US hyperscaler HPC projects.
Risks to Downside
n China's HPC localization progress slows down.
n AI chipset progress is much slower to develop.
n NRE demand decelerates.
n Pricing competition intensifies.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Update
ASE Technology Holding Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
Asia AI Summit 2026 Feedback
ASE AI summit takeaways:
• Utilization: ASE’s ATM 1Q utilization is ~80%. 2Q improves to 80%+, with
85% as full utilization.
• Capex: 2026 capex is expected at US$8.5bn: US$3bn for buildings and
infrastructure, and US$5.5bn for machinery and equipment. Within
equipment, testing accounts for 40%, packaging 55%, and the rest EMS.
Management expects 2027 capex to at least match 2026 and could be

higher.
• Margin: Management expects ATM gross margins to remain within the
structural 24–30% range through 2026, improving each quarter, with 2H
reaching the upper end.
• CPO opportunities:
The company has a mini line for CPO and is part of the
supply chain.
• CoPoS: A few products are in qualification, but mass production timing is not
clear at this stage.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Update
Silergy Corp.  | Asia Pacific
Asia   AI Summit 2026 Feedback
Silergy AI Summit takeaways:
• 2026 full-year guidance: Management expect 20%+ Y/Y revenue growth in
2026, driven by data center and auto. 2H Y/Y growth is expected to
accelerate vs. 1H.
• Margin outlook: GM declined in 1Q on inventory impairment, lower auto
mix, and higher foundry costs. Management expect 1Q26 margins to mark
the yearly bottom.
• End-market mix: Consumer 36%, automotive 13%, communication 6%,

computer 14%, industrial 33% in 1Q26.
• Data center products:
Enterprise SSD, optical module, and general server
motherboards. Data center products span industrial and compute, ~15% of
mix in 1Q26; management expect ~20% by year-end 2026.
• 8-inch/12-inch mix:
In 2025, >90% of wafers were 8-inch. By year-end 2026,
>20% will be 12-inch.
• Auto semi localization in China: The company benefits from industry
consolidation and expanding global market share of Chinese auto makers.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Silergy Corp. (6415.TW)
Base case, residual income model. We assume a cost of equity of 9.5% (2.0% risk-free rate, 6%
risk premium, beta of 1.25), an intermediate growth rate of 13.5%, and a terminal growth rate
of 4.5%.
Risks to Upside
n Gross margin expansion amid stabilized competition
n A surge in LED demand as pricing drops to the sweet spot
n Greater-than-expected benefits from China's support in local semiconductors

n Higher-than-expected dividend payout ratio
Risks to Downside
n Lower gross margin amid intensified competition
n Weaker-than-expected benefits from China's support in local semiconductors
n Lower-than-expected dividend payout ratio

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 095: 260601_daiwa_Fositek

Source PDF: all report pdf/260601_daiwa_Fositek.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-06-01
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Fositek joined our Daiwa Corporate Day on 29 May and 
shared an update on its business. We expect the company to benefit from 
the AI server trend in the coming years, with rising server revenue from 
QDs and slides over 2026-28E and share-gain potential in GPU and ASIC 
applications. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating. See our previous flash, Server 
segment should be the key earnings driver, 7 May 2026.

What’s the impact: Positive tone from the investor conference. For 
2Q26, Fositek guided revenue to be flat to up 5% QoQ, with double-digit  
QoQ revenue growth in both 3Q26 and 4Q26. We believe 2H26 revenue 
should be stronger than 1H26, supported by more ASIC projects, VR, LPX 
and Vera CPU projects. For CPO, Fositek is sampling QDs to CSP and 
GPU clients, which is likely to contribute revenue in 2027, according to 
management. Apart from AVC (see our latest flash on 14 May), Fositek is 
also testing with other thermal solution vendors, as well as new segments 
such as power rack. For slides, the company plans to tap into the liquid 
cooling segment in 2H26, with target revenue set to more than double YoY 
in 2026. For hinges for foldable phones, Fositek aims for flat YoY revenue 
in 2026, with the launch of three-fold phones in 3Q26 in a sole-supplier 
position. The company is also positive on the 2027 outlook, thanks to rising 
liquid cooling applications in VR and ASICs. It sees a high utilisation rate 
for QDs, with intact order visibility (without product transition concerns), and 
we believe there could be some upside potential to gross margin on 
improving yield rates and automation levels. On the gross margin side, 
Fositek expects to grow QoQ in 2026. We slightly cut our 2026E EPS by 
2% on more conservative revenue assumptions, but raise our 2027-28E 
EPS by 4-5% on our more aggressive revenue and gross margin 
assumptions. Note: Its product mix for 1Q26 is as below: foldable phone 
37.7% (-4.7% YoY), notebook 5.2% (+6.2% YoY), server 56.4% (+546.9% 
YoY) and others 0.7% (+8.4% YoY); its April 2026 product mix is: foldable 
phone 40.2%, notebook 5.7%, server 53.5% and others 0.6%

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call with an unchanged 
12M TP of TWD2,600, still based on a target PER of 33x (vs. its past-3-
year range of 9-43x [average of 23x]), translating to a 0.42x PEG over 
2025-27E on our 1-year forward EPS. Key downside risks: lower-than-
expected market share for key clients; slower-than-expected penetration 
rate of foldable phones; and geopolitical risks.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 1-7% above Bloomberg 
consensus, likely as we are more positive on its revenue and gross 
margins.

Company profile 
Fositek is a provider of foldable phone hinges, foldable laptop/tablet hinges, AR/VR glasses hinges, 
ultra-thin laptop hinges and keyboard hinges, as well as hinges for medical instruments and automobile 
applications. Its major clients include Huawei and Motorola.

Style Exposure
• MLCC UTR could rise fast in coming months. YAGEO does not have the
leadership in the high capacitance/voltage MLCCs, which are used heavily in

AI server systems. However, as much of its major peers' capacity is tied up with
these fast-growing AI products, which can consume as much as 3-5x
production resources vs regular MLCCs, we expect the overflow orders to lift
YAGEO's IT MLCC UTR by 10ppt in 1H (YoY), and further increased to 15ppt
YoY in 2H (hence we now expect 85-90% peak IT UTR for YAGEO to be
reached in 2H; while auto/industrial MLCC UTR shall also improve on YoY
basis). We also believe that, due to the AI-driven high UTR and B/B ratios of

the industry, the potential consumer demand impact (if the segment weakens)
should be manageable. As a rule of thumb, the key factor behind the pricing of
passive component is the respective UTR of the product.
• General IT MLCC could see a bit of pricing surprise. On the pricing side,
while the major AI MLCC suppliers in Japan and Korea have not finalized the
price hike decisions, the JPM team expects an increasingly constructive pricing
environment. It is crucial to understand that the price hike timing and

momentum for general IT (non-AI) MLCCs could surprise on the upside, as
hikes could take place earlier/rise in stronger magnitude vs the AI MLCCs. We
believe many suppliers would leverage the industry supply tightness to
improve the mix and/or the relatively low margins of these non-AI
applications, where the pricing initiative could start with non-AI products.
Please keep in mind we have seen this situation occurr for memory, PCB and
CCL in the past few years, and this could result in strong incremental

improvements in profitability for general IT MLCCs, a situation that we
believe is favorable for YAGEO.
• AI story much beyond MLCC : Tantalum entering multi-year up-cycle.
We believe YAGEO has a bit of a different AI story vs. its peers, which mainly
produce MLCCs. YAGEO’s revenue mix is largely filled with non-MLCC
products such as Tantalum (No.1 product by rev mix for YAGEO at ~25/30%
in 2026/27e), power inductors (25-30% of YAGEO’s total inductors, which are

its No.2 product by revenue) and resistors (14/15% of YAGEO revenue in
2026/27e). This could see a very rapid rise in AI mix, and/or see stronger
pricing momentum as YAGEO can exert much more clout on pricing vs.
MLCCs, which are largely determined by Japanese producers (which have a
tendency to maintain stable pricing). We expect tantalum’s double-digit ASP
growth in recent years will likely accelerate as AI quickly becomes 35-40% of
its mix (driven by the need for power management and data storage). Investors

Summary Investment Thesis and Valuation
Investment Thesis
We are OW on YAGEO, given its leading position in tantalum
capacitors after acquiring Kemet and its strong presence in
MLCCs/R-Chips, a market that has consolidated over time with
disciplined supply expansion. We believe less exposure to
commodity-type products can help smooth the revenue
fluctuations, while increasing demand for tantalum capacitors

can support YAGEO’s future top- and bottom-line growth.
Valuation
Our Dec-26 PT of NT$1,000 is based on 26x 2027/28E EPS. 26x
is beyond 1.5-STD above average forward P/E at which the
company has traded since 2021. We believe the multiple is
justified as passive component enters demand-driven upcycle,
which is more structural and healthy versus the previous cycle
in 2018-19. We believe YAGEO is one of the key beneficiaries
of this cycle with 1) dominance in high-end passives (global

leader in Tantalum), and 2) MLCC price hike starting from the
lower-end segment.

(behind Tantalum and inductor), has drew much less attention. We believe resistors,
which have a high correlation in usage vs. MLCC, could be a major incremental OP
contributor with comparable impact to Tantalum in the coming years, as we see healthy
pricing upside. YAGEO has quite a dominating global share (40% by volume, which is
a very high level in the world of passive components), while the product has gone through

many years of modest pricing (hence it currently makes up a tiny small portion of the
passive BOM despite its indispensable role), the growing demand now likely pushes
YAGEO’s resistor UTR to north of 90% in the upcoming peak season. Given these
favorable conditions, we expect resistor pricing, which saw a successful hike in 1Q26,
to increase by 15-20% CAGR in the coming years and further improve its already decent
profitability.
• Lifting TP to NT$1,000, based on 26x of average 2027/28e EPS. YAGEO is currently

trading at 48/27x 2026/27e EPS, which is meaningfully below its Japanese and Korean
peers, despite 1) comparably strong earnings growth (with potential upside if price
adjustment becomes stronger vs. our assumptions of ASP showing a ~15% CAGR in the
coming 2-3 years), and 2) similar exposure to AI/HPC (but with much more diverse
product exposure). Our new target multiple, at 26x (was 19x which was above 1.5-STD
above mean; previous TP based on 2027e), is slightly above the 23-24x forward earnings

multiple YAGEO traded at during the 2018-19 peak cycle. While there are some
similarities between the previous cycle vs the current one (EV/industrial caused supply
tightness vs. AI) we believe such re-rating is deserving as this margin expansion cycle
could last even longer as most of the major suppliers seem to have taken mix improvement
as their long term goal, which leads more rational capacity expansions. We also believe
the AI driven demand will moderate the pace of component size miniaturization, hence

positive to the industry supply/demand balance.

J P M O R G A N
Investment Thesis, Valuation and Risks
YAGEO (Overweight; Price Target: NT$1,000.00)
Investment Thesis
We are OW on YAGEO, given its leading position in tantalum capacitors after acquiring
Kemet and its strong presence in MLCCs/R-Chips, a market that has consolidated over time
with disciplined supply expansion. We believe less exposure to commodity-type products

can help smooth the revenue fluctuations, while increasing demand for tantalum capacitors
can support YAGEO’s future top- and bottom-line growth.
Valuation
Our Dec-26 PT of NT$1,000 is based on 26x 2027/28E EPS. 26x is beyond 1.5-STD above
average forward P/E at which the company has traded since 2021. We believe the multiple
is justified as passive component enters demand-driven upcycle, which is more structural
and healthy versus the previous cycle in 2018-19. We believe YAGEO is one of the key

beneficiaries of this cycle with 1) dominance in high-end passives (global leader in
Tantalum), and 2) MLCC price hike starting from the lower-end segment.
Risks to Rating and Price Target
Downside risks include MLCC/R-Chip price reversals (which could be driven by a slower
pace of auto electrification, inventory corrections, etc.) and execution issues during
mergers.

J.P. Morgan ratings or designations: OW = Overweight, N= Neutral, UW = Underweight, NR = Not Rated
Explanation of Equity Research Ratings, Designations and Analyst(s) Coverage Universe:
J.P. Morgan uses the following rating system: Overweight (over the duration of the price target indicated in this report, we expect this stock will

outperform the average total return of the stocks in the Research Analyst’s, or the Research Analyst’s team’s, coverage universe); Neutral (over
the duration of the price target indicated in this report, we expect this stock will perform in line with the average total return of the stocks in the
Research Analyst’s, or the Research Analyst’s team’s, coverage universe); and Underweight (over the duration of the price target indicated in
this report, we expect this stock will underperform the average total return of the stocks in the Research Analyst’s, or the Research Analyst’s

team’s, coverage universe. NR is Not Rated. In this case, J.P. Morgan has removed the rating and, if applicable, the price target, for this stock
because of either a lack of a sufficient fundamental basis or for legal, regulatory or policy reasons. The previous rating and, if applicable, the
price target, no longer should be relied upon. An NR designation is not a recommendation or a rating. Some stocks under coverage have a rating
but no price target; in these cases, we expect the stock will outperform/perform in line/underperform the average total return of the stocks in the

the certifying Research Analyst’s coverage universe can be found on J.P. Morgan’s Research website, https://www.jpmorganmarkets.com.

Update
WinWay Technology Co Ltd  | Asia Pacific
Asia   AI Summit 2026 Feedback
Larger package size drives testing interface demand: As testing time continues to
increase, the pin count per socket has increased from 4-6k to over 10k, and
management expects this trend to  continue in the next few years as chips become
more and more complex. Incremental testing steps, such as functional burn-in or
SL T, also expand the TAM for Winway.
Burn-in socket:  This was not the key focus for Winway in the past, but it said it will

now try
to target the high-end segment instead of dealing with pricing competition
in the low-end market once it expands its  new capacity in the next few years.
Capacity expansion plan: Winway expects its pogo pin capacity for test sockets to
reach 9mn/month by end-2026, then 14mn/month by 1H27, mainly from its Renwu
Facility 1. Renwu Facility 2 is expected to start in 2Q27, further lifting capacity to
14mn/month. The current pogo-pin self-sufficiency ratio is ~35-45%; management

Valuation Methodology and Risks
WinWay Technology Co Ltd (6515.TW)
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture
long-term value. We assume a 9.14% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium
5.5%), a payout ratio of 80%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.4%, and a terminal growth rate
of 4.5%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected adoption of hypersockets and MEMS probe cards

n Faster-than-expected SL T and burn-in socket introduction
n Stronger-than-expected edge AI demand
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected adoption of hypersockets and MEMS probe cards
n Slower-than-expected SL T and burn-in socket introduction
n No edge AI demand in the next few years

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Airtac
Valuation
We derive our target price of NT$1,750 for Airtac by using a P/E methodology, as we believe the margin and earnings growth
outlooks will be the key drivers of the stock. We apply a P/E of 30x for 2Q26-1Q27E, which we derive from the mid range of prior
upcycle valuation 25-35x during 2021 in view of a strong upcycle with solid FA demand and ongoing share gains.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the Airtac stock from reaching our target price include: 1) a softer-than-expected China

macro economy, resulting in downside to our sales forecasts; 2) intensifying competition leading to gross margin pressures; and
3) slower-than-expected progress of its linear guide business, causing low utilization of capacity.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C.
Hon Hai (2317.TW): Computex 2026: AI servers platform capabilities
showcased; New AI infrastructure projects in Europe; Buy (on CL)
2 June 2026 | 10:53PM HKT
We visited the Hon Hai booth at Computex on June 2, where the company
showcased its AI servers and high-speed interconnect solution in various
architecture, optical products (e.g. ELSFP for CPO switch, 1.6T optical modules),
Space data center solution, and AI models/ Agents system. The company

demonstrated its comprehensive product offerings, manuf acturing, and system
integration capabilities for AI infrastructure, and also highlighted its AI server
deployment through local partnerships (for example, new AI data center projects in
Europe). We remain positive on Hon Hai, given its strong R&D capabilities, global
supply chain with scale advantages, and migration to the full AI ecosystem
integration. Maintain Buy (on CL).
Key takeaways
1. AI server and high-speed interconnect solution: Hon Hai showcased its AI

servers and interconnect solution in various architecture, including Vera Rubin
NVL72 rack-level AI servers, HGX Rubin NVL8 AI severs, Groq 3 LPX AI servers, and
the Spectrum-X SN6600 Ethernet switch, demonstrating the company’s
manufacturing and system integration capabilities. Management is positive on the AI
servers ramp up on the growing Gen-AI penetration and rising AI inferencing
demand. Besides the full system, the company also demonstrated the AI server

components including thermal components (3D VC, manifold), optical components
(ELSFP , optical modules) etc., reflecting its vertical integration.
2. AI Infrastructure solution in Europe: Hon Hai announced a strategic partnership
with leading AI computing supplier Bull to build the AI and Cloud infrastructure from
Europe to the global market. The AI deployment in France is expected to cost over
EUR 120m initially. We are positive on company’s global manufacturing scale and

supply chain capabilities to support Sovereign AI projects. Meanwhile, Hon Hai has
also partnered with Radiall and Thales on a plan to produce “made in Europe”
components with large scale (Link).

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

## Document 114: 260602_gs_TUC

Source PDF: all report pdf/260602_gs_TUC.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-06-02
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C., Taipei
Branch
Taiwan Union Technology Corp. (6274.TWO): Computex & Corp Day:
High-end demand accelerating in 2H26; adding M7+ capacity with
2 June 2026 | 11:31PM CST
We hosted meetings with TUC at our Taiwan Computex & Corporate Day (June
2nd). Key takeaways include:
(1) Higher price hike on low-end CCL to release more capacity for high-end CCL:
Management mentioned price increase for lower-end CCL is higher, with the main

objective to discourage low-end CCL demand (~18% of total revenue in 1Q26),
enabling the company to release capacity for more high-end CCL. The transitioning
will reduce output by ~10%, but the potential for significantly higher revenue (M7+
CCL ASP is generally 100%+ higher than M6 below grade CCL ASP) and GM makes
the strategy economically attractive per TUC.
(2) Capacity expansion continues but lagging PCB expansion growth: The
company mentioned that it will add another 300K sheets/month of capacity by

3Q26 end (due to the equipment constraints issue, the company’s new 3Q26
capacity, which originally was planned to ramp up in early 3Q, will be delayed by 2
months, per the company), and additional capacity is scheduled throughout 2027,
with around 30-35% YoY capacity growth. Despite ongoing CCL capacity expansion,
CCL supply remains constrained as PCB capacity growth continues to outpace.
(3) High-end product mix to continue to go up in 2H26: TUC is positive on the

demand for its ASIC AI server project in 2H26, as customers seek OOC (out-of-China)
capacity, TUC is well positioned to gain share once the additional capacity in
Thailand is ready. The company has gained share in computing and switch board
(with M8 CCL) for a new US customer. Furthermore, the company is also seeing some
server CPU projects starting to adopt M8 CCL (vs. consensus mostly on M6/7, based
on our supply chain checks).
(4) 1.6T switch to ramping up in 2H26: The ramp up of 800G/1.6T switch in 2H26 is

expected to adopt M8 CCL with low-DK2 glass fiber, while TUC believe the M9 CCL
solutions could be massively adopted for 1.6T switch in 2027, as customers express
interest in higher-end spec. Furthermore, the company is currently qualifying optical
modules for 800G/1.6T using M8 CCL with low-DK2 glass fiber.
(5) More opportunities in heavy copper CCL: TUC has exposure to heavy copper
CCL (~11% of total revenue in 1Q26), which is mainly for PSU and demand remains

strong. For HVDC in the AI datacenter industry, TUC is seeing opportunities in heavy
copper adoption, supported by spec upgrades from 3oz to 6oz to improve thermal
resistance; moreover, the company maintains its constructive view on its market
share in AI HVDC industry (basically 100% for now and foreseeable future), and
Goldman Sachs does and seeks to do business with companies covered in its research reports. As a result, investors
should be aware that the firm ma y have a conflict of interest that could affect the objectivity of this report.

www.gs.com/research/hedge.html
. Analysts employed by non-US affiliates are not registered/qualified as research
analysts with FINRA in the U.S.
Equity Research
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 2

believes its market leader position can be secured by its good position in the supply
chain (especially the HVDC copper foil industry) in the long term, despite the capacity
shortage issue now.
(6) Expansion in Thailand to meet OOC demand: TUC continues to focus on expanding
its Thailand capacity. Although CCL pricing in Thailand is higher, which is in line with the
PCB pricing trends in the region, multiple customers are willing to accept this price

premium due to strict OOC (out-of-China) sourcing requirements.
Investment view
We are positive on the company’s pricing strategy to clear out low-end CCL capacity and
priortize high-end demand. We believe low-end CCL orders could see cancellations due
to the higher pricing, but this would suggest more capacity for TUC to work with
high-end/AI customers, and this shift could support better ASP and GM (high-end AI
project ASP can be 3-5x higher than low end projects, and GM can be 40%+ vs. low end

only at 5-15%). We expect TUC to continue to gain share in the AI industry (we believe
the company will have at least 4+ mainstream AI project in 2027E). The company’s
proactive capacity expansion plans (65% capacity expansion in the next 18 months) to
support new AI project demand is in line with our positive view on its expanding market
share outlook.
TUC is a key high-end global CCL supplier that focuses on M7+ grade high-speed CCL
(key applications include high-end switches (100G+) and AI server materials), with 20%+

market share in the past two years. The company continues to expect its unit market
share in the server industry to increase from 15% in Whitley and Purley processors to
higher in the Eagle Stream generation, and plans to launch two types of products for
low-end and high-end customers (T2A and T2C) to gain market share. Also, strong
growth in 400G/800G switch shipments and new 800G switch shipments, scheduled for
launch in 2025-27, should continue to be a key demand driver for TUC, in our view.

Additionally, the company is working on high-end AI projects with CSP and enterprise
players, which we believe should drive revenue momentum in the long term. We view the
stock as undervalued vs. Taiwan CCL peers on a P/E basis and rate it Buy.
Key downside risks include: (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL
segment; (2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch
shipments globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$1,888 is based on a 22x 2027E P/E (+2x
STDV higher than the past 3-year industry average P/E multiple).
Key downside risks:  (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL segment;
(2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch shipments
globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C.
Wiwynn (6669.TW): Computex 2026: CPO in AI server racks scale up;
Buy
2 June 2026 | 11:01PM HKT
We visited Wiwynn booth on June 2 during our Computex tour in Taipei. Wiwynn
showcased its AI server solution across multiple architecture, optical interconnect
solution, HVDC (High-voltage Power Architecture), and advanced cooling solution.
The company highlights its CPO ecosystem with industry partners (Ayar Labs,
Browave, FOCI, Senko etc.) and management is positive on the AI scale-up

opportunities to lower the power consumption and support the interconnection. We
remain positive on the company’s expansion across ASIC/ GPU clients, with ongoing
AI server racks specifica tion upgrade with liquid-cooled and CPO in scale up.
Maintain Buy.
Key takeaways
1. High-performance AI platform and cooling solution: Wiwynn showcased the
NVIDIA Vera Rubin NVL72 and AMD Helios rack-level solution for AI training/
inferencing, and also provides storage server prototype to enable GPU-direct

storage access. On cooling, management notes the company offers 6kW-class
double-sided liquid cooling solution with Diamond Composite based microchannel
design for the high-power AI systems. We are positive on the company’s capabilities
of full rack integration by leveraging joint-design with Wistron, and diversified client
base across GPU/ ASIC platform.
2. Positive on CPO opportunities: The company is positive on the rising CPO trend
for AI scale-up through the collaboration with industry partners. Wiwynn has built

the strategic partnership with Ayar Labs through the integration of Ayar Labs’ optical
engines and ELSFP light sources into its rack-level architecture to deliver
high-bandwidth and power efficient CPO solution. Management also highlights the
company’s in-house liquid cooling solution to support the stable and consistent laser
output of the ELS (External Light Source).

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
Agentic AI and CPU servers dominated the Computex keynote
discussions. Nvidia indicated that TSMC’s capacity can support
robust growth for Nvidia in 2027.
Agentic AI compute key focus: In their keynotes, Nvidia, Arm and Qualcomm

emphasized Arm-based server CPUs to support future agentic AI demand, echoing
our report, Global Tech: Agentic AI – The Surge Begins (11 May 2026).
Comments on Nvidia Vera CPU: Ahead of Computex, we published NVIDIA's Vera
CPU and Rubin GPU seen as the main show (27 May 2026). At today’s investor
luncheon, our Nvidia analyst Joe Moore asked CEO Jensen Huang the first question
about the US$20bn Vera CPU revenue assumption. Management indicated that

demand spans both AI server head nodes and stand-alone CPU servers. Given its
materially better performance than the x86 (see Exhibit 5 ), Nvidia can command
higher values for Vera, excl. memory sales. Our supply chain checks suggest 2.5mn–
4mn units are in preparation. Rubin has already entered fab production with no
apparent delays. On CPO (optics) vs. copper, Nvidia indicated that it will adopt CPO
only when necessary and rely on copper as long as possible.

Sufficient TSMC capacity:
At the 2024 and 2025 Computex events, we asked
Jensen Huang about TSMC’s pricing and value. This year, we asked whether Nvidia
can secure sufficient TSMC capacity for 2027 and whether allocation could affect
market share. We also asked – even more importantly – how Nvidia could secure
incremental allocation versus competitors.
Nvidia indicated it has sufficient TSMC capacity to support robust growth in 2027.
The company has convinced TSMC and other suppliers that demand remains strong.

Even as supply expands, demand will likely still exceed supply in 2027. Management
also emphasized that the AI semiconductor market is growing and does not
represent a zero-sum or pure market share competition.
Agenetic AI at the edge –  do we need a local LLM/agent:
Nvidia announced its RTX
Sparks AI PC WoA SoC in its keynote, jointly designed with MediaTek. We flagged
this 9 months ago in our MediaTek report, saying “Nvidia/MediaTek WoA AI PC keeps

being pushed out – now to 2H26. For N1X, we think the new timing of product launch
would be next year's Computex in June (vs. the CES in January), given that Microsoft's
new AI Windows system will be more of a 2H26 rollout.
Moreover, the new x86 SoC
will fuse an Intel CPU + Nvidia RTX GPU chiplet via NVLink". We do not see this
launch as a surprise. Investors are asking about the volume of RTX Sparks in 2026
and whether edge AI compute is necessary for Agentic AI.

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
